HELPER FUNCTION: setAtomicToNewer() (line 2049)\n\n**Location:** Lines 2046-2064\n\n```go\nfunc setAtomicToNewer(value *atomic.Int64, newValue int64) (previous int64, updated bool) {\n    for {\n        current := value.Load()\n        if current >= newValue {\n            return current, false  // Don't downdate timestamps\n        }\n        if value.CompareAndSwap(current, newValue) {\n            return current, true  // Successfully updated to newer value\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nUsed for atomically updating timestamps to the newer of two values (prevents going backwards).\n\n---\n\n### 11. HELPER FUNCTION: setAtomicToNewer() (line 2049)\n\n**Location:** Lines 2046-2064\n\n```go\nfunc setAtomicToNewer(value *atomic.Int64, newValue int64) (previous int64, updated bool) {\n    for {\n        current := value.Load()\n        if current >= newValue {\n            return current, false  // Don't downdate timestamps\n        }\n        if value.CompareAndSwap(current, newValue) {\n            return current, true  // Successfully updated to newer value\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nUsed for atomically updating timestamps to the newer of two values (prevents going backwards).\n\n---\n\n### 11. dataOutRate\n\t\thighestSent     = t.metrics.highestSentTimestamp.Get()\n\t\thighestRecv     = t.metrics.highestTimestamp.Get()\n\t\tdelay           = highestRecv - highestSent\n\t\tdataPending     = delay * dataInRate\n\t)\n\n\tif dataOutRate <= 0 {\n\t\treturn t.numShards\n\t}\n\n\tvar (\n\t\t// When behind we will try to catch up on 5% of samples per second.\n\t\tbacklogCatchup = 0.05 * dataPending\n\t\t// Calculate Time to send one sample, averaged across all sends done this tick.\n\t\ttimePerSample = dataOutDuration / dataOutRate\n\t\tdesiredShards = timePerSample * (dataInRate + backlogCatchup)\n\t)\n\tt.metrics.desiredNumShards.Set(desiredShards)\n\tt.logger.Debug(\"QueueManager.calculateDesiredShards\",\n\t\t\"dataInRate\", dataInRate,\n\t\t\"dataOutRate\", dataOutRate,\n\t\t\"dataPendingRate\", dataPendingRate,\n\t\t\"dataPending\", dataPending,\n\t\t\"dataOutDuration\", dataOutDuration,\n\t\t\"timePerSample\", timePerSample,\n\t\t\"desiredShards\", desiredShards,\n\t\t\"highestSent\", highestSent,\n\t\t\"highestRecv\", highestRecv,\n\t)\n\n\t// Changes in the number of shards must be greater than shardToleranceFraction.\n\tvar (\n\t\tlowerBound = float64(t.numShards) * (1. - shardToleranceFraction)\n\t\tupperBound = float64(t.numShards) * (1. + shardToleranceFraction)\n\t)\n\tt.logger.Debug(\"QueueManager.updateShardsLoop\",\n\t\t\"lowerBound\", lowerBound, \"desiredShards\", desiredShards, \"upperBound\", upperBound)\n\n\tdesiredShards = math.Ceil(desiredShards) // Round up to be on the safe side.\n\tif lowerBound <= desiredShards && desiredShards <= upperBound {\n\t\treturn t.numShards\n\t}\n\n\tnumShards := int(desiredShards)\n\t// Do not downshard if we are more than ten seconds back.\n\tif numShards < t.numShards && delay > 10.0 {\n\t\tt.logger.Debug(\"Not downsharding due to being too far behind\")\n\t\treturn t.numShards\n\t}\n\n\tswitch {\n\tcase numShards > t.cfg.MaxShards:\n\t\tnumShards = t.cfg.MaxShards\n\tcase numShards < t.cfg.MinShards:\n\t\tnumShards = t.cfg.MinShards\n\t}\n\treturn numShards\n}\n\nfunc (t *QueueManager) reshardLoop() {\n\tdefer t.wg.Done()","numLines":75,"startLine":1111,"totalLines":2231}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01H1jgfVB4vjdi6RSFRhcduE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011Lqkiu9ZpM1DonE9zjrFQG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/investigation.md","content":"# Investigation Report\n\n## Summary\n\nCommit `184c7eb91` moved `dataIn` rate tracking from `WriteStorage.timestampTracker.Commit()` into `shards.enqueue()`, creating a deadlock during shard scale-up: when all shard queues are full, `enqueue()` blocks in a retry loop holding `shards.mtx.RLock`, which prevents `reshardLoop` -> `shards.stop()` from acquiring the write lock needed to drain queues and start new shards, while `calculateDesiredShards()` cannot tick `dataIn` because `updateShardsLoop` is blocked trying to send on `reshardChan`. This was confirmed and reverted in commit `6806b68f9` (PR #17412, issue #17384).\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe root cause is a **circular lock/channel dependency** introduced by commit `184c7eb91` (\"storage/remote: compute highestTimestamp and dataIn at QueueManager level\"). Two interrelated problems create the deadlock:\n\n### Problem 1: dataIn.tick() starvation causes runaway shard calculation\n\nBefore `184c7eb91`, `dataIn` was a shared `*ewmaRate` created by `WriteStorage` and ticked by a dedicated `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine every `shardUpdateDuration`. After `184c7eb91`:\n\n- `dataIn` is created per-`QueueManager` (`queue_manager.go:518`)\n- `dataIn.incr(1)` is called inside `shards.enqueue()` (`queue_manager.go:1338`)\n- `dataIn.tick()` is called inside `calculateDesiredShards()` (`queue_manager.go:1112`)\n- The `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine was **removed** (`write.go` diff)\n\nThis means `dataIn.tick()` is **only** called when `calculateDesiredShards()` runs (every `shardUpdateDuration` via `updateShardsLoop`). If `updateShardsLoop` is blocked (e.g., because `reshardChan` was full from a previous cycle), `dataIn` accumulates events without ticking, leading to an inflated `dataInRate` on the next tick. This causes `calculateDesiredShards()` to compute an artificially high shard count, triggering unnecessary scale-up reshards.\n\n### Problem 2: Deadlock during shard scale-up with full queues\n\nThe actual deadlock occurs through this sequence:\n\n1. **Queues fill up**: Remote endpoint is slow (e.g., 1-second response times). With `capacity: 1` (or small capacity), the per-shard `queue.batchQueue` channel fills. `queue.Append()` returns `false`.\n\n2. **Enqueue retry loop blocks**: `QueueManager.Append()` (`queue_manager.go:731-755`) enters a retry loop calling `shards.enqueue()` repeatedly. Each call to `enqueue()` acquires `shards.mtx.RLock()` (`queue_manager.go:1313`). The WAL watcher goroutine is stuck in this retry loop, continuously holding the RLock.\n\n3. **Resharding is triggered**: `updateShardsLoop` calculates a higher shard count (amplified by Problem 1) and sends on `reshardChan` (`queue_manager.go:1072`).\n\n4. **reshardLoop calls stop()**: `reshardLoop` receives the new shard count and calls `shards.stop()` (`queue_manager.go:1193`). The `stop()` method:\n   - Acquires `shards.mtx.RLock()` to close `softShutdown` channel (`queue_manager.go:1274-1276`)\n   - Then tries to acquire `shards.mtx.Lock()` (write lock) at `queue_manager.go:1281`\n\n5. **DEADLOCK**: The write lock at step 4 **cannot be acquired** because the WAL watcher goroutine(s) from step 2 are continuously re-acquiring `shards.mtx.RLock()` in their retry loops. Even though `softShutdown` is closed (which would make `enqueue()` return `false`), there is a **race window**: the retry loop checks `<-s.softShutdown` via `select/default` (`queue_manager.go:1316-1319`), but between the `default` branch being taken and the `s.mtx.RLock()` at line 1313, another goroutine can close `softShutdown`. The retrying goroutine already holds RLock and proceeds to try `queue.Append()`, which fails, then loops back and re-acquires RLock again -- **before** the `stop()` goroutine can acquire the write lock.\n\n   In Go's `sync.RWMutex`, a pending writer blocks new readers only **after** a writer calls `Lock()`. But the retry loop goroutines can re-enter RLock between the softShutdown close (line 1275, under RLock) and the writer Lock attempt (line 1281). If there are multiple retry goroutines (multiple WAL watcher goroutines for multiple Append methods), they can \"leapfrog\" each other holding RLock, starving the writer indefinitely.\n\n6. **Stalled shards**: The old shards cannot be stopped (blocked at write lock), new shards cannot be started, and samples accumulate with `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending` stuck at >0 with no progress.\n\n### Why the original design worked\n\nIn the pre-`184c7eb91` code:\n- `dataIn` was a shared `*ewmaRate` passed from `WriteStorage`, ticked by a separate goroutine in `WriteStorage.run()`\n- `dataIn.incr()` was called in `timestampTracker.Commit()` (at the appender level), not inside `enqueue()`\n- `dataDropped` tracked relabelled-away samples, and `dataKeptRatio` corrected the shard calculation\n- The shard calculation was more conservative, reducing unnecessary scale-up triggers\n\n## Evidence\n\n### Problematic commit: `184c7eb91`\n- Removed `dataDropped` EWMA rate from `QueueManager` struct (`queue_manager.go:455` diff)\n- Removed `highestRecvTimestamp` field from `QueueManager` (`queue_manager.go:458` diff)\n- Removed `samplesIn *ewmaRate` parameter from `NewQueueManager` (`queue_manager.go:469` diff)\n- Created `dataIn` per-queue instead of sharing: `dataIn: newEWMARate(ewmaWeight, shardUpdateDuration)` (`queue_manager.go:518` diff)\n- Moved `dataIn.tick()` into `calculateDesiredShards()` (`queue_manager.go:1112`)\n- Added `dataIn.incr(1)` inside `shards.enqueue()` (`queue_manager.go:1338`)\n- Removed `dataDropped.incr(1)` from all drop paths in Append methods\n- Simplified shard formula from `dataPendingRate = dataInRate*dataKeptRatio - dataOutRate` to `dataPendingRate = dataInRate - dataOutRate` (`queue_manager.go:1124`)\n- Removed `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine and `quit` channel from `write.go`\n\n### Revert commit: `6806b68f9` (PR #17412, issue #17384)\n- Restored `dataDropped` field on `QueueManager` (`queue_manager.go:453`)\n- Restored `highestRecvTimestamp` field (`queue_manager.go:459`)\n- Restored `samplesIn` and `highestRecvTimestamp` parameters to `NewQueueManager`\n- Restored `dataKeptRatio` in `calculateDesiredShards()` (`queue_manager.go:1145`)\n- Removed `dataIn.incr(1)` from `shards.enqueue()` (`queue_manager.go:1360` diff)\n- Removed `dataIn.tick()` from `calculateDesiredShards()` (tick is done by `WriteStorage.run()`)\n- Restored `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine, `samplesIn` field, and `quit` channel in `write.go`\n- Restored `samplesIn.incr()` in `timestampTracker.Commit()` (`write.go:348` diff)\n- Added `TestRemoteWrite_ReshardingWithoutDeadlock` test (`cmd/prometheus/main_test.go:968`)\n\n### Key code locations (current HEAD, i.e., the buggy version):\n\n| Location | Description |\n|---|---|\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1338` | `dataIn.incr(1)` inside `shards.enqueue()` -- called under `shards.mtx.RLock` |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1112` | `dataIn.tick()` inside `calculateDesiredShards()` -- only tick location |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1124` | Simplified formula `dataPendingRate = dataInRate - dataOutRate` (no `dataKeptRatio`) |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1313` | `s.mtx.RLock()` in `enqueue()` |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1316-1319` | `softShutdown` check via `select/default` (race window) |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:731-755` | Retry loop in `QueueManager.Append()` calling `enqueue()` |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1274-1276` | `stop()` closes `softShutdown` under RLock |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1281` | `stop()` attempts write lock (blocked by RLock holders) |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1193` | `reshardLoop` calls `shards.stop()` then `shards.start()` |\n| `storage/remote/write.go:60-78` | `WriteStorage` struct -- no `samplesIn` or `quit` field in current code |\n| `storage/remote/write.go:331-337` | `timestampTracker.Commit()` -- no `samplesIn.incr()` call in current code |\n| `storage/remote/ewma.go:51-65` | `ewmaRate.tick()` -- resets `newEvents` counter and updates EWMA |\n| `storage/remote/ewma.go:68-70` | `ewmaRate.incr()` -- atomically adds to `newEvents` |\n\n## Affected Components\n\n- **`storage/remote/queue_manager.go`**: `QueueManager`, `shards`, `calculateDesiredShards()`, `reshardLoop()`, `updateShardsLoop()`, `enqueue()`, `stop()`, `start()`\n- **`storage/remote/write.go`**: `WriteStorage`, `timestampTracker`, `NewWriteStorage()`, `ApplyConfig()`\n- **`storage/remote/ewma.go`**: `ewmaRate` -- `tick()` / `incr()` separation is central to the bug\n- **`cmd/prometheus/main_test.go`**: Integration test `TestRemoteWrite_ReshardingWithoutDeadlock` (added in revert)\n- **Downstream impact**: Any Prometheus deployment using remote-write with endpoints that have variable latency or capacity constraints\n\n## Recommendation\n\n### Fix Strategy\n\nApply the revert from commit `6806b68f9` (already available in the repo history). This restores three critical design properties:\n\n1. **Shared `dataIn` rate with independent ticking**: `dataIn` (`samplesIn`) is created in `WriteStorage`, shared across all `QueueManager` instances, and ticked by an independent `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine. This decouples the tick from the shard calculation cycle, preventing `dataInRate` inflation.\n\n2. **`dataIn.incr()` at appender commit, not in enqueue**: Moving the incr call to `timestampTracker.Commit()` removes the contention path where `incr()` is called under `shards.mtx.RLock`, eliminating the deadlock precondition.\n\n3. **`dataKeptRatio` correction**: Restoring `dataDropped` and the `dataKeptRatio = dataOutRate / (dataDropped.rate() + dataOutRate)` formula prevents over-estimation of incoming data rate when relabelling drops a significant fraction of series, reducing unnecessary shard scale-ups.\n\n### Diagnostic Steps to Confirm the Issue\n\n1. **Check for stalled shards**: Query `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending > 0` combined with `rate(prometheus_remote_storage_samples_total[5m]) == 0`. Pending samples with zero send rate indicates stalled shards.\n\n2. **Check for resharding events**: Look for log lines `\"Resharding queues\"` followed by `\"Resharding done\"`. If resharding logs stop appearing (or `\"Currently resharding, skipping.\"` appears repeatedly), the reshardLoop is blocked.\n\n3. **Check desired vs actual shards**: Compare `prometheus_remote_storage_shards_desired` with `prometheus_remote_storage_shards`. A large desired-to-actual gap indicates the resharding mechanism is stuck.\n\n4. **Check goroutine dump**: A goroutine dump during the stall will show:\n   - WAL watcher goroutine(s) blocked in `shards.enqueue()` retry loop\n   - `reshardLoop` goroutine blocked at `shards.stop()` -> `s.mtx.Lock()`\n   - `updateShardsLoop` goroutine blocked trying to send on `reshardChan`\n\n5. **Check remote endpoint latency**: `prometheus_remote_storage_sent_batch_duration_seconds` with high p99 values correlate with queues filling up, which is the precondition for the deadlock.\n\n6. **Monitor `prometheus_remote_storage_shards_desired` spikes**: Sudden large increases in desired shards (due to inflated `dataInRate` from infrequent ticking) are a leading indicator of the issue.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":103,"cache_read_input_tokens":57442,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":103,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"19442777-79ac-4d62-abd3-41a12189f373","uuid":"31e12bd9-fd18-43dd-8d4d-3f3bbfcf8853"}

dataOutRate` to `dataPendingRate = dataInRate - dataOutRate` (`queue_manager.go:1124`)\n- Removed `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine and `quit` channel from `write.go`\n\n### Revert commit: `6806b68f9` (PR #17412, issue #17384)\n- Restored `dataDropped` field on `QueueManager` (`queue_manager.go:453`)\n- Restored `highestRecvTimestamp` field (`queue_manager.go:459`)\n- Restored `samplesIn` and `highestRecvTimestamp` parameters to `NewQueueManager`\n- Restored `dataKeptRatio` in `calculateDesiredShards()` (`queue_manager.go:1145`)\n- Removed `dataIn.incr(1)` from `shards.enqueue()` (`queue_manager.go:1360` diff)\n- Removed `dataIn.tick()` from `calculateDesiredShards()` (tick is done by `WriteStorage.run()`)\n- Restored `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine, `samplesIn` field, and `quit` channel in `write.go`\n- Restored `samplesIn.incr()` in `timestampTracker.Commit()` (`write.go:348` diff)\n- Added `TestRemoteWrite_ReshardingWithoutDeadlock` test (`cmd/prometheus/main_test.go:968`)\n\n### Key code locations (current HEAD, i.e., the buggy version):\n\n| Location | Description |\n|---|---|\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1338` | `dataIn.incr(1)` inside `shards.enqueue()` -- called under `shards.mtx.RLock` |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1112` | `dataIn.tick()` inside `calculateDesiredShards()` -- only tick location |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1124` | Simplified formula `dataPendingRate = dataInRate - dataOutRate` (no `dataKeptRatio`) |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1313` | `s.mtx.RLock()` in `enqueue()` |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1316-1319` | `softShutdown` check via `select/default` (race window) |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:731-755` | Retry loop in `QueueManager.Append()` calling `enqueue()` |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1274-1276` | `stop()` closes `softShutdown` under RLock |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1281` | `stop()` attempts write lock (blocked by RLock holders) |\n| `storage/remote/queue_manager.go:1193` | `reshardLoop` calls `shards.stop()` then `shards.start()` |\n| `storage/remote/write.go:60-78` | `WriteStorage` struct -- no `samplesIn` or `quit` field in current code |\n| `storage/remote/write.go:331-337` | `timestampTracker.Commit()` -- no `samplesIn.incr()` call in current code |\n| `storage/remote/ewma.go:51-65` | `ewmaRate.tick()` -- resets `newEvents` counter and updates EWMA |\n| `storage/remote/ewma.go:68-70` | `ewmaRate.incr()` -- atomically adds to `newEvents` |\n\n## Affected Components\n\n- **`storage/remote/queue_manager.go`**: `QueueManager`, `shards`, `calculateDesiredShards()`, `reshardLoop()`, `updateShardsLoop()`, `enqueue()`, `stop()`, `start()`\n- **`storage/remote/write.go`**: `WriteStorage`, `timestampTracker`, `NewWriteStorage()`, `ApplyConfig()`\n- **`storage/remote/ewma.go`**: `ewmaRate` -- `tick()` / `incr()` separation is central to the bug\n- **`cmd/prometheus/main_test.go`**: Integration test `TestRemoteWrite_ReshardingWithoutDeadlock` (added in revert)\n- **Downstream impact**: Any Prometheus deployment using remote-write with endpoints that have variable latency or capacity constraints\n\n## Recommendation\n\n### Fix Strategy\n\nApply the revert from commit `6806b68f9` (already available in the repo history). This restores three critical design properties:\n\n1. **Shared `dataIn` rate with independent ticking**: `dataIn` (`samplesIn`) is created in `WriteStorage`, shared across all `QueueManager` instances, and ticked by an independent `WriteStorage.run()` goroutine. This decouples the tick from the shard calculation cycle, preventing `dataInRate` inflation.\n\n2. **`dataIn.incr()` at appender commit, not in enqueue**: Moving the incr call to `timestampTracker.Commit()` removes the contention path where `incr()` is called under `shards.mtx.RLock`, eliminating the deadlock precondition.\n\n3. **`dataKeptRatio` correction**: Restoring `dataDropped` and the `dataKeptRatio = dataOutRate / (dataDropped.rate() + dataOutRate)` formula prevents over-estimation of incoming data rate when relabelling drops a significant fraction of series, reducing unnecessary shard scale-ups.\n\n### Diagnostic Steps to Confirm the Issue\n\n1. **Check for stalled shards**: Query `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending > 0` combined with `rate(prometheus_remote_storage_samples_total[5m]) == 0`. Pending samples with zero send rate indicates stalled shards.\n\n2. **Check for resharding events**: Look for log lines `\"Resharding queues\"` followed by `\"Resharding done\"`. If resharding logs stop appearing (or `\"Currently resharding, skipping.\"` appears repeatedly), the reshardLoop is blocked.\n\n3. **Check desired vs actual shards**: Compare `prometheus_remote_storage_shards_desired` with `prometheus_remote_storage_shards`. A large desired-to-actual gap indicates the resharding mechanism is stuck.\n\n4. **Check goroutine dump**: A goroutine dump during the stall will show:\n   - WAL watcher goroutine(s) blocked in `shards.enqueue()` retry loop\n   - `reshardLoop` goroutine blocked at `shards.stop()` -> `s.mtx.Lock()`\n   - `updateShardsLoop` goroutine blocked trying to send on `reshardChan`\n\n5. **Check remote endpoint latency**: `prometheus_remote_storage_sent_batch_duration_seconds` with high p99 values correlate with queues filling up, which is the precondition for the deadlock.\n\n6. **Monitor `prometheus_remote_storage_shards_desired` spikes**: Sudden large increases in desired shards (due to inflated `dataInRate` from infrequent ticking) are a leading indicator of the issue.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
